            <script type="text/javascript">

                function sendNps(nota){
                    
                    fetch(`/php/atualizaNPS.php?nota=${nota}`)
                        .then(data => data.json())
                        .then((data) => {
                            if (data.result[0].erro == "0") {
                                showNps = false;
                                move('#nps-bottom')
                                    .y(0)
                                    .duration(1000)
                                    .end();

                                toastr.success(data.result[0].resultado);   
                            }
                        });
                }

                setTimeout(() => {
                    if (showNps && isGestor === 1){
                        if (localStorage.getItem("hasShowNPS") === null){
                            localStorage.setItem("hasShowNPS", true);
                            move('#nps-bottom')
                                .y(-250)
                                .duration(1000)
                                .end();
                        }
                    } else {
                        localStorage.removeItem("hasShowNPS");
                    }
                    
                }, 6000);

                $(".btnRemoveNPS").on('click',() => {
                    move('#nps-bottom')
                        .y(0)
                        .duration(1000)
                        .end();                    
                });

         </script>

            <script type="text/javascript">$(".btn-caixa").click(function(event){shroom=$("#caixaSide");if(shroom.hasClass('open')){shroom.removeClass('open');move('#caixaSide').x(0).duration(250).end();}else{shroom.addClass('open');move('#caixaSide').x(-650).duration(250).end();}});$(".sidebar-menu").click(function(){if($("#caixaSide").hasClass('open')){$("#caixaSide").removeClass('open');move('#caixaSide').x(0).duration(250).end();}});</script>
